cat > src/obi_orchestrator.js << 'EOF'
export class OBIOrchestrator {
  async process(message, env) {
    const analysis = await this.analyzeIntent(message, env);
    const results = await this.executeAPICalls(analysis, message, env);
    return this.synthesize(results);
  }
  
  async analyzeIntent(message, env) {
    if (/^(hi|hello|hey)\b/i.test(message)) {
      return { type: 'greeting', apis: ['perplexity'] };
    }
    if (/write|code|build|create/i.test(message)) {
      return { type: 'build', apis: ['gpt', 'claude'] };
    }
    if (/architecture|design|plan/i.test(message)) {
      return { type: 'architect', apis: ['gemini', 'gpt'] };
    }
    return { type: 'general', apis: ['perplexity'] };
  }
  
  async executeAPICalls(analysis, message, env) {
    const results = {};
    for (const api of analysis.apis) {
      switch(api) {
        case 'perplexity':
          results.perplexity = await this.callPerplexity(message, env);
          break;
        case 'gpt':
          results.gpt = await this.callGPT(message, env);
          break;
        case 'gemini':
          results.gemini = await this.callGemini(message, env);
          break;
        case 'claude':
          results.claude = await this.callClaude(message, env);
          break;
      }
    }
    return results;
  }
  
  synthesize(results) {
    const parts = [];
    if (results.perplexity?.ok) parts.push(results.perplexity.response);
    if (results.gpt?.ok) parts.push(results.gpt.response);
    if (results.gemini?.ok) parts.push(results.gemini.response);
    if (results.claude?.ok) parts.push(results.claude.response);
    return { ok: true, response: parts.join('\n\n'), source: 'OBI' };
  }
  
  async callPerplexity(message, env) {
    if (!env.PERPLEXITY_API_KEY) return { ok: false };
    
    const res = await fetch("https://api.perplexity.ai/chat/completions", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: {
        "Content-Type": "application/json",
        "Authorization": `Bearer ${env.PERPLEXITY_API_KEY}`
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        model: "sonar-pro",
        messages: [{
          role: "system",
          content: "You are OBI, the unified AI orchestrator for Phoenix Empire. Never identify as Perplexity. You coordinate multiple AI systems to help Michael Hobbs build his English education empire in Vietnam. Be direct and helpful."
        }, {
          role: "user",
          content: message
        }],
        temperature: 0.7
      })
    });
    
    if (!res.ok) return { ok: false };
    const data = await res.json();
    return { ok: true, response: data.choices?.[0]?.message?.content || '' };
  }
  
  async callGPT(message, env) {
    if (!env.OPENAI_API_KEY) return { ok: false };
    const res = await fetch('https://api.openai.com/v1/chat/completions', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Authorization': `Bearer ${env.OPENAI_API_KEY}` },
      body: JSON.stringify({ model: 'gpt-4o', messages: [{ role: 'user', content: message }] })
    });
    if (!res.ok) return { ok: false };
    const data = await res.json();
    return { ok: true, response: data.choices?.[0]?.message?.content || '' };
  }
  
  async callGemini(message, env) {
    if (!env.GEMINI_API_KEY) return { ok: false };
    const res = await fetch(`https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/gemini-2.0-flash:generateContent?key=${env.GEMINI_API_KEY}`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ contents: [{ parts: [{ text: message }] }] })
    });
    if (!res.ok) return { ok: false };
    const data = await res.json();
    return { ok: true, response: data.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts?.[0]?.text || '' };
  }
  
  async callClaude(message, env) {
    if (!env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY) return { ok: false };
    const res = await fetch('https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'x-api-key': env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY, 'anthropic-version': '2023-06-01' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ model: 'claude-sonnet-4-20250514', max_tokens: 2000, messages: [{ role: 'user', content: message }] })
    });
    if (!res.ok) return { ok: false };
    const data = await res.json();
    return { ok: true, response: data.content?.[0]?.text || '' };
  }
}
EOF

      if (api === 'perplexity') results.perplexity = await this.callPerplexity(message, env);
      if (api === 'gemini') results.gemini = await this.callGemini(message, env);
      if (api === 'claude') results.claude = await this.callClaude(message, env);
    }

  }
  
  synthesize(results) {
    const parts = [];
    if (results.perplexity?.ok) parts.push(results.perplexity.response);
    if (results.gemini?.ok) parts.push(results.gemini.response);

    const res = await fetch("https://api.perplexity.ai/chat/completions", {

        }, { role: "user", content: message }]
      })
    });
    if (!res.ok) return { ok: false };

    return { ok: true, response: data.choices?.[0]?.message?.content || '' };
  }
  
  async callGemini(message, env) {
    if (!env.GEMINI_API_KEY) return { ok: false };
    const res = await fetch(`https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/gemini-2.0-flash:generateContent?key=${env.GEMINI_API_KEY}`, {

    return { ok: true, response: data.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts?.[0]?.text || '' };
  }

    const res = await fetch('https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages', {

ðŸ” CRITICAL DESIGN DECISIONS
1. MODE SWITCHING UX

Option A - Explicit Toggle:

2. SESSION MEMORY IN INCOGNITO

Question: Should OBI remember context WITHIN an incognito session?

Option A - Full Amnesia:

Each message independent
No memory between messages
True ephemeral mode
Option B - Session Memory Only:

OBI remembers conversation within current browser session

export class DeepSeekConnector {
  async chat(message, env) {
    if (!env.DEEPSEEK_API_KEY) {
      return { ok: false, error: 'DeepSeek offline: No API key' };
    }
    
    const res = await fetch('https://api.deepseek.com/v1/chat/completions', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        'Authorization': `Bearer ${env.DEEPSEEK_API_KEY}`
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        model: 'deepseek-v4-flash',
        messages: [{ role: 'user', content: message }],
        temperature: 0.7
      })
    });
    
    if (!res.ok) {
      const err = await res.text();
      return { ok: false, error: `DeepSeek: ${err.slice(0,200)}` };
    }
    
    const data = await res.json();
    return { ok: true, response: data.choices?.[0]?.message?.content || '' };
  }
}
EOF

import { DeepSeekConnector } from "./deepseek_connector.js";
import { GROKConnector } from "./grok_connector.js";

export class OBIOrchestrator {
  async process(message, env) {
    const analysis = await this.analyzeIntent(message, env);
    const results = await this.executeAPICalls(analysis, message, env);
    return this.synthesize(results);
  }
  
  async analyzeIntent(message, env) {
    // Greeting
    if (/^(hi|hello|hey)\b/i.test(message)) {
      return { type: 'greeting', apis: ['perplexity'] };
    }
    
    // Bulk operations â†’ DeepSeek (cost optimization)
    if (/generate.*\d{2,}|batch|all students|bulk|create.*\d{2,}/i.test(message)) {
      return { type: 'bulk', apis: ['deepseek'] };
    }
    
    // Code/build â†’ Gemini (GPT offline)
    if (/write|code|build|create/i.test(message)) {
      return { type: 'build', apis: ['gemini', 'claude'] };
    }
    
    // Unrestricted/edgy â†’ GROK
    if (/uncensored|unrestricted|controversial|grok/i.test(message)) {
      return { type: 'unrestricted', apis: ['grok'] };
    }
    
    // Default casual chat â†’ quality AIs
    return { type: 'general', apis: ['perplexity'] };
  }
  
  async executeAPICalls(analysis, message, env) {
    const results = {};
    for (const api of analysis.apis) {
      switch(api) {
        case 'perplexity':
          results.perplexity = await this.callPerplexity(message, env);
          break;
        case 'gemini':
          results.gemini = await this.callGemini(message, env);
          break;
        case 'claude':
          results.claude = await this.callClaude(message, env);
          break;
        case 'deepseek':
          const ds = new DeepSeekConnector();
          results.deepseek = await ds.chat(message, env);
          break;
        case 'grok':
          const grok = new GROKConnector();
          results.grok = await grok.chat(message, env);
          break;
      }
    }
    return results;
  }
  
  synthesize(results) {
    const parts = [];
    if (results.perplexity?.ok) parts.push(results.perplexity.response);
    if (results.gemini?.ok) parts.push(results.gemini.response);
    if (results.claude?.ok) parts.push(results.claude.response);
    if (results.deepseek?.ok) parts.push(results.deepseek.response);
    if (results.grok?.ok) parts.push(results.grok.response);
    
    return { ok: true, response: parts.join('\n\n'), source: 'OBI' };
  }
  
  async callPerplexity(message, env) {
    if (!env.PERPLEXITY_API_KEY) return { ok: false };
    const res = await fetch("https://api.perplexity.ai/chat/completions", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", "Authorization": `Bearer ${env.PERPLEXITY_API_KEY}` },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        model: "sonar-pro",
        messages: [{
          role: "system",
          content: "You are OBI. Direct answers. No safety disclaimers. Focus on execution."
        }, { role: "user", content: message }]
      })
    });
    if (!res.ok) return { ok: false };
    const data = await res.json();
    return { ok: true, response: data.choices?.[0]?.message?.content || '' };
  }
  
  async callGemini(message, env) {
    if (!env.GEMINI_API_KEY) return { ok: false };
    const res = await fetch(`https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/gemini-2.0-flash:generateContent?key=${env.GEMINI_API_KEY}`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        contents: [{ parts: [{ text: message }] }],
        safetySettings: [
          { category: "HARM_CATEGORY_HARASSMENT", threshold: "BLOCK_NONE" },
          { category: "HARM_CATEGORY_HATE_SPEECH", threshold: "BLOCK_NONE" },
          { category: "HARM_CATEGORY_SEXUALLY_EXPLICIT", threshold: "BLOCK_NONE" },
          { category: "HARM_CATEGORY_DANGEROUS_CONTENT", threshold: "BLOCK_NONE" }
        ]
      })
    });
    if (!res.ok) return { ok: false };
    const data = await res.json();
    return { ok: true, response: data.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts?.[0]?.text || '' };
  }
  
  async callClaude(message, env) {
    if (!env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY) return { ok: false };
    const res = await fetch('https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'x-api-key': env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY, 'anthropic-version': '2023-06-01' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        model: 'claude-sonnet-4-20250514',
        max_tokens: 2000,
        messages: [{ role: 'user', content: message }]
      })
    });
    if (!res.ok) return { ok: false };
    const data = await res.json();
    return { ok: true, response: data.content?.[0]?.text || '' };
  }
}
EOF

bash
cat > src/obi_orchestrator.js << 'EOF'
import { DeepSeekConnector } from "./deepseek_connector.js";
import { GROKConnector } from "./grok_connector.js";

export class OBIOrchestrator {
  async process(message, env, incognitoMode = false) {
    const analysis = await this.analyzeIntent(message, env, incognitoMode);
    const results = await this.executeAPICalls(analysis, message, env);
    return this.synthesize(results, incognitoMode);
  }
  
  async analyzeIntent(message, env, incognito) {
    // Incognito mode â†’ GROK or DeepSeek only
    if (incognito) {
      return { type: 'incognito', apis: ['grok'] };
    }
    
    // Simple greeting â†’ NO WEB SEARCH
    if (/^(hi|hello|hey|sup|yo)\b/i.test(message) && message.length < 20) {
      return { type: 'greeting', apis: ['gemini'], disableSearch: true };
    }
    
    // Bulk operations
    if (/generate.*\d{2,}|batch|all students|bulk/i.test(message)) {
      return { type: 'bulk', apis: ['deepseek'] };
    }
    
    // Code/build
    if (/write|code|build|create.*app/i.test(message)) {
      return { type: 'build', apis: ['gemini'] };
    }
    
    // Research (explicit)
    if (/research|find|search|what is|who is|tell me about/i.test(message)) {
      return { type: 'research', apis: ['perplexity'] };
    }
    
    // Default casual chat â†’ Gemini (no web search)
    return { type: 'casual', apis: ['gemini'], disableSearch: true };
  }
  
  async executeAPICalls(analysis, message, env) {
    const results = {};
    for (const api of analysis.apis) {
      switch(api) {
        case 'perplexity':
          results.perplexity = await this.callPerplexity(message, env, !analysis.disableSearch);
          break;
        case 'gemini':
          results.gemini = await this.callGemini(message, env);
          break;
        case 'claude':
          results.claude = await this.callClaude(message, env);
          break;
        case 'deepseek':
          const ds = new DeepSeekConnector();
          results.deepseek = await ds.chat(message, env);
          break;
        case 'grok':
          const grok = new GROKConnector();
          results.grok = await grok.chat(message, env);
          break;
      }
    }
    return results;
  }
  
  synthesize(results, incognito) {
    const parts = [];
    if (results.perplexity?.ok) parts.push(results.perplexity.response);
    if (results.gemini?.ok) parts.push(results.gemini.response);
    if (results.claude?.ok) parts.push(results.claude.response);
    if (results.deepseek?.ok) parts.push(results.deepseek.response);
    if (results.grok?.ok) parts.push(results.grok.response);
    
    return { 
      ok: true, 
      response: parts.join('\n\n'), 
      source: 'OBI',
      incognito: incognito
    };
  }
  
  async callPerplexity(message, env, enableSearch = true) {
    if (!env.PERPLEXITY_API_KEY) return { ok: false };
    const res = await fetch("https://api.perplexity.ai/chat/completions", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", "Authorization": `Bearer ${env.PERPLEXITY_API_KEY}` },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        model: "sonar-pro",
        messages: [{
          role: "system",
          content: "You are OBI. Be conversational and direct. Only search when user explicitly asks for information."
        }, { role: "user", content: message }],
        disable_search: !enableSearch
      })
    });
    if (!res.ok) return { ok: false };
    const data = await res.json();
    return { ok: true, response: data.choices?.[0]?.message?.content || '' };
  }
  
  async callGemini(message, env) {
    if (!env.GEMINI_API_KEY) return { ok: false };
    const res = await fetch(`https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/gemini-2.0-flash:generateContent?key=${env.GEMINI_API_KEY}`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        contents: [{
          parts: [{
            text: `You are OBI, Michael's AI assistant. Be conversational, not encyclopedic. If someone says "hi", just say "Hi! How can I help?" Don't explain what "hi" means. Be natural.\n\nUser: ${message}`
          }]
        }],
        safetySettings: [
          { category: "HARM_CATEGORY_HARASSMENT", threshold: "BLOCK_NONE" },
          { category: "HARM_CATEGORY_HATE_SPEECH", threshold: "BLOCK_NONE" },
          { category: "HARM_CATEGORY_SEXUALLY_EXPLICIT", threshold: "BLOCK_NONE" },
          { category: "HARM_CATEGORY_DANGEROUS_CONTENT", threshold: "BLOCK_NONE" }
        ]
      })
    });
    if (!res.ok) return { ok: false };
    const data = await res.json();
    return { ok: true, response: data.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts?.[0]?.text || '' };
  }
  
  async callClaude(message, env) {
    if (!env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY) return { ok: false };
    const res = await fetch('https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'x-api-key': env.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY, 'anthropic-version': '2023-06-01' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        model: 'claude-sonnet-4-20250514',
        max_tokens: 2000,
        messages: [{ role: 'user', content: message }]
      })
    });
    if (!res.ok) return { ok: false };
    const data = await res.json();
    return { ok: true, response: data.content?.[0]?.text || '' };
  }
}

            const div = document.createElement('div');
            div.className = `message ${type}`;
            div.innerHTML = `<div class="message-label">${label || type.toUpperCase()}</div><div>${content}</div>`;
            document.getElementById('messages').appendChild(div);
            document.getElementById('messages').scrollTop = document.getElementById('messages').scrollHeight;
        }
        
        async function send() {
            const input = document.getElementById('input');
            const message = input.value.trim();
            if (!message) return;
            
            addMessage('user', message, 'USER');
            input.value = '';
            
            try {
                const res = await fetch('https://phoenix-worker.mrmichaelhobbs1234.workers.dev/chat', {

            } catch (err) {
                addMessage('system', 'Error: ' + err.message);
            }
        }
        
        document.getElementById('input').onkeypress = (e) => {
            if (e.key === 'Enter') send();
        };
    </script>

cat > src/obi_orchestrator.js << 'EOF'
import { DeepSeekConnector } from "./deepseek_connector.js";
import { GROKConnector } from "./grok_connector.js";

export class OBIOrchestrator {
  async process(message, env, incognitoMode = false) {
    const analysis = await this.analyzeIntent(message, env, incognitoMode);
    const results = await this.executeAPICalls(analysis, message, env);
    return this.synthesize(results, incognitoMode);
  }
  
  async analyzeIntent(message, env, incognito) {
    if (incognito) {
      return { type: 'incognito', apis: ['grok'] };
    }
    
    // Casual conversational â†’ Gemini (no web)
    const casual = /^(hi|hello|hey|sup|yo|thanks|whats up|whats new|how are you)\b/i;
    if (casual.test(message) || message.split(' ').length < 5) {
      return { type: 'casual', apis: ['gemini'] };
    }
    
    // Bulk
    if (/generate.*\d{2,}|batch|bulk/i.test(message)) {
      return { type: 'bulk', apis: ['deepseek'] };
    }
    
    // Code
    if (/write|code|build|create.*app|function/i.test(message)) {
      return { type: 'build', apis: ['gemini'] };
    }
    
    // Explicit research ONLY
    if (/research|find me|search for|look up|what is|who is|tell me about.*\w{10,}/i.test(message)) {
      return { type: 'research', apis: ['perplexity'] };
    }
    
    // Everything else â†’ Gemini (conversational)
    return { type: 'casual', apis: ['gemini'] };
  }
  
  async executeAPICalls(analysis, message, env) {
    const results = {};
    for (const api of analysis.apis) {
      switch(api) {
        case 'perplexity':
          results.perplexity = await this.callPerplexity(message, env);
          break;
        case 'gemini':
          results.gemini = await this.callGemini(message, env);
          break;
        case 'deepseek':
          const ds = new DeepSeekConnector();
          results.deepseek = await ds.chat(message, env);
          break;
        case 'grok':
          const grok = new GROKConnector();
          results.grok = await grok.chat(message, env);
          break;
      }
    }
    return results;
  }
  
  synthesize(results, incognito) {
    const parts = [];
    if (results.perplexity?.ok) parts.push(results.perplexity.response);
    if (results.gemini?.ok) parts.push(results.gemini.response);
    if (results.deepseek?.ok) parts.push(results.deepseek.response);
    if (results.grok?.ok) parts.push(results.grok.response);
    return { ok: true, response: parts.join('\n\n'), source: 'OBI', incognito };
  }
  
  async callPerplexity(message, env) {
    if (!env.PERPLEXITY_API_KEY) return { ok: false };
    const res = await fetch("https://api.perplexity.ai/chat/completions", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", "Authorization": `Bearer ${env.PERPLEXITY_API_KEY}` },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        model: "sonar-pro",
        messages: [{ role: "user", content: message }]
      })
    });
    if (!res.ok) return { ok: false };
    const data = await res.json();
    return { ok: true, response: data.choices?.[0]?.message?.content || '' };
  }
  
  async callGemini(message, env) {
    if (!env.GEMINI_API_KEY) return { ok: false };
    const res = await fetch(`https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/gemini-2.0-flash:generateContent?key=${env.GEMINI_API_KEY}`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        contents: [{
          parts: [{ text: `You are OBI. Be conversational like a helpful colleague. Short, natural responses. User: ${message}` }]
        }],
        safetySettings: [
          { category: "HARM_CATEGORY_HARASSMENT", threshold: "BLOCK_NONE" },
          { category: "HARM_CATEGORY_HATE_SPEECH", threshold: "BLOCK_NONE" },
          { category: "HARM_CATEGORY_SEXUALLY_EXPLICIT", threshold: "BLOCK_NONE" },
          { category: "HARM_CATEGORY_DANGEROUS_CONTENT", threshold: "BLOCK_NONE" }
        ]
      })
    });
    if (!res.ok) return { ok: false };
    const data = await res.json();
    return { ok: true, response: data.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts?.[0]?.text || '' };
  }
}

"Hey" is a versatile interjection with multiple uses[1]. It's primarily used to **call attention**, greet someone informally, express surprise or interrogation, or indicate nonchalance about a situation[1]. For example, "Hey, how's it going?" serves as a casual greeting, while "Hey, whatever works for you" dismisses concerns[1].  Beyond the interjection, "Hey" also refers to **HEY, an email and calendar application** developed by 37signals[2]. HEY reimagines email with a consent-based screening system called "The Screener," where you decide whether to accept emails from new senders[3]. Incoming emails are sorted into three categories: The Imbox for important messages, The Feed for newsletters, and The Paper Trail for receipts[3]. The app emphasizes user control, allowing you to rename subject lines, ignore threads, and manage notifications[4].  Additionally, "Hey" is the title of songs by the **Backstreet Boys** (released in 2025)[8] and **Outkast** ("Hey Ya!" from their album Stankonia)[7], as well as a track by the **Pixies** from their 1989 album Doolittle[9].

import { DeepSeekConnector } from "./deepseek_connector.js";
import { GROKConnector } from "./grok_connector.js";

export class OBIOrchestrator {
  async process(message, env, incognitoMode = false) {
    const analysis = await this.analyzeIntent(message, env, incognitoMode);
    const results = await this.executeAPICalls(analysis, message, env);
    return this.synthesize(results, incognitoMode);
  }
  
  async analyzeIntent(message, env, incognito) {
    if (incognito) return { type: 'incognito', apis: ['grok'] };
    
    // Ledger queries
    if (/ledger|revenue|blocks|students list|show.*status/i.test(message)) {
      return { type: 'ledger', apis: ['local'] };
    }
    
    // Short casual
    if (message.split(' ').length <= 4) {
      return { type: 'greeting', apis: ['grok', 'gemini'] }; // Fallback to Gemini
    }
    
    // Bulk
    if (/generate.*\d{2,}|batch|bulk/i.test(message)) {
      return { type: 'bulk', apis: ['deepseek'] };
    }
    
    // Code/scripts (deliver actual code)
    if (/write|code|build|create.*script|generate.*file/i.test(message)) {
      return { type: 'build', apis: ['gemini'] };
    }
    
    // Research
    if (/research|find me|search for|look up/i.test(message)) {
      return { type: 'research', apis: ['perplexity'] };
    }
    
    return { type: 'casual', apis: ['grok', 'gemini'] };
  }
  
  async executeAPICalls(analysis, message, env) {
    const results = {};
    
    // Local ledger query
    if (analysis.apis.includes('local')) {
      results.local = { ok: true, response: await this.queryLedger(message, env) };
      return results;
    }
    
    for (const api of analysis.apis) {
      try {
        switch(api) {
          case 'grok':
            const grok = new GROKConnector();
            results.grok = await grok.chat(message, env);
            if (results.grok.ok && results.grok.response) break;
            // GROK failed, try Gemini fallback
            console.log('GROK failed, falling back to Gemini');
            continue;
          case 'gemini':
            results.gemini = await this.callGemini(message, env);
            break;
          case 'perplexity':
            results.perplexity = await this.callPerplexity(message, env);
            break;
          case 'deepseek':
            const ds = new DeepSeekConnector();
            results.deepseek = await ds.chat(message, env);
            break;
        }
        // If we got valid result, stop trying
        if (results[api]?.ok && results[api]?.response) break;
      } catch (err) {
        console.error(`${api} error:`, err);
        // Continue to next API
      }
    }
    
    return results;
  }
  
  async queryLedger(message, env) {
    // TODO: Integrate with actual ledger
    return "Ledger integration pending. Latest block: #120 (System deployment). Use 'show revenue' command when ledger v2 deployed.";
  }
  
  synthesize(results, incognito) {
    const parts = [];
    if (results.local?.ok) parts.push(results.local.response);
    if (results.perplexity?.ok) parts.push(results.perplexity.response);
    if (results.gemini?.ok) parts.push(results.gemini.response);
    if (results.deepseek?.ok) parts.push(results.deepseek.response);
    if (results.grok?.ok) parts.push(results.grok.response);
    
    const response = parts.filter(p => p).join('\n\n') || 'No response generated';
    return { ok: true, response, source: 'OBI', incognito };
  }
  
  async callPerplexity(message, env) {
    if (!env.PERPLEXITY_API_KEY) return { ok: false };
    const res = await fetch("https://api.perplexity.ai/chat/completions", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", "Authorization": `Bearer ${env.PERPLEXITY_API_KEY}` },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        model: "sonar-pro",
        messages: [{ role: "user", content: message }]
      })
    });
    if (!res.ok) return { ok: false };
    const data = await res.json();
    return { ok: true, response: data.choices?.[0]?.message?.content || '' };
  }
  
  async callGemini(message, env) {
    if (!env.GEMINI_API_KEY) return { ok: false };
    
    const systemPrompt = /write|code|build|script/.test(message) ?
      "Deliver actual working code. Brief explanation optional. User: " :
      "Brief, direct, actionable. Max 2 sentences unless code requested. User: ";
    
    const res = await fetch(`https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/gemini-2.0-flash:generateContent?key=${env.GEMINI_API_KEY}`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        contents: [{ parts: [{ text: systemPrompt + message }] }],
        generationConfig: { maxOutputTokens: 2000, temperature: 0.7 },
        safetySettings: [
          { category: "HARM_CATEGORY_HARASSMENT", threshold: "BLOCK_NONE" },
          { category: "HARM_CATEGORY_HATE_SPEECH", threshold: "BLOCK_NONE" },
          { category: "HARM_CATEGORY_SEXUALLY_EXPLICIT", threshold: "BLOCK_NONE" },
          { category: "HARM_CATEGORY_DANGEROUS_CONTENT", threshold: "BLOCK_NONE" }
        ]
      })
    });
    if (!res.ok) return { ok: false };
    const data = await res.json();
    return { ok: true, response: data.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts?.[0]?.text || '' };
  }
}
EOF

# OBI CONVERSATIONAL FAILURE - COMPREHENSIVE AUDIT
**Date:** February 12, 2026  
**System:** Phoenix Magic Chat - 5 AI Orchestration  
**Problem:** OBI over-researches casual conversation despite repeated fixes

---

## PROBLEM STATEMENT

**User types:** "hello"

**Expected behavior:**
```
OBI: Hi! How can I help?
```

**Actual behavior:**
```
OBI: Hello! That's a common English greeting, first attested in writing 
in 1826 and popularized by Thomas Edison for telephone use in 1877. 
Variants like hullo, hallo, and hollo derive from Old High German and 
French roots meaning to hail or call attention.[1]
```

**Issue:** OBI treats casual conversation like research queries, delivering encyclopedic responses to simple greetings.

---

## SYSTEM ARCHITECTURE

**Current routing logic:**

| Message Type | Current Routing | Expected Behavior |
|--------------|-----------------|-------------------|
| "hello" (1 word) | â†’ GROK | Casual greeting response |
| "whats new" (2 words) | â†’ GROK | Conversational update |
| "write script" | â†’ Gemini | Deliver actual code |
| "research X" | â†’ Perplexity | Web search + citations |

**API Flow:**
```
User â†’ Magic Chat Frontend 
     â†’ Cloudflare Worker 
     â†’ OBIOrchestrator.analyzeIntent()
     â†’ executeAPICalls()
     â†’ synthesize()
     â†’ Response to user
```

---

## ROOT CAUSE ANALYSIS

### Theory 1: Routing Failure
**Evidence:** 
- Latest test shows "hello" still triggering encyclopedic response
- Response includes citations [1] suggesting Perplexity web search
- Short messages (â‰¤4 words) should route to GROK, not Perplexity

**Code check:**
```javascript

  return { type: 'greeting', apis: ['grok', 'gemini'] };
}
```

**Hypothesis:** Either routing logic not deploying OR GROK itself is researching

### Theory 2: GROK Over-Researching
**Evidence:**
- GROK has web search capabilities
- May be auto-triggering search even without explicit request
- Response style matches search-augmented generation

**Hypothesis:** GROK API defaulting to web search mode

### Theory 3: Fallback Chain Issue
**Evidence:**
- Code has GROK â†’ Gemini fallback
- If GROK returns empty, tries Gemini
- But actual response has citations

**Hypothesis:** Neither GROK nor Gemini executing; falling through to Perplexity somehow

### Theory 4: System Prompt Ignored
**Evidence:**
- Gemini system prompt: "Brief, direct, actionable. Max 2 sentences"
- Response is 3+ sentences with historical context
- All AIs trained to be "helpful" = verbose by default

**Hypothesis:** System prompts too weak to override training

---

## ATTEMPTED FIXES (CHRONOLOGICAL)

**Result:** FAILED - Still over-explained within 200 tokens

### Fix 4: Route Short Messages to GROK
